### Orc Pirates

- Stub: Booma Pistol, 20 gc, Common; its rules are on p4.
- Flag (p2): This PDF is two warbands, the Orc Pirates (p2-4) and the Savage Orcs (p5-7), each "the exact same rules as the Da Mob Warband with the following changes": William, 11 Nov 2026: "Orc Pirates & Savage Orcs should just be 2 variants of the orc mob if that still works, correctly pointing at their settings". So this page is not a warband list of its own: it folds into the Orc Mob's template as one of its variants (`variantOf`), which serves the Orc Mob (Mordheim 1a), the Orc Pirates and the Savage Orcs (Sartosa), each type presetting its own variant. Its rules are its variant's, its lists' lines name it, and its fighters are the variant's: one Da Mob lacks joins the roster and no other variant allows any of him, and one it lacks is allowed none in this variant.
- Flag (p3): The PDF prints no skill table for the Orc Pirates, so their heroes tick no Special column of their own and name our Orcs group, as Da Mob's do.
- Flag (p2): The PDF prints no Pirate column and no Pirate skill list, so this warband does not join the shared Sartosa Pirate Skills group. See the decisions.
- Flag (p3): The Boss, the Big 'Uns and the Orc Boyz are "same as Da Mob", so their costs, profiles, starting experience, counts and skill access are our Orc Mob template's. The Orc Shaman is kept, since the Goblin Enjuneer replaces him rather than removing him; our templates hold no replacement rule.
- Flag (p3): The Goblin Enjuneer's skills are "Combat, Shooting, Speed. He may also learn the Orc Skill Waaagh", so he ticks no Special column of his own and names our Orcs group; the other heroes name it too, as Da Mob's do.
- Flag (p4): The Sea Troll's Regeneration (a 4+ to ignore a wound) is not the Bounty of Arkhar mutation Regeneration (heal to full Wounds), so it is kept apart as "Regeneration (Sea Troll)". The Sea Squig and the Sea Troll are the Cave Squig and the Troll of Da Mob with the PDF's changes, at their Da Mob costs (15 gc and 200 gc, "the normal amount"). The Sea Troll loses the Vomit Attack and gains Aquatic and Slimy.
- Flag (p4): A Sea Squig moves 2D6-1" on land and swims 2D6". Our profile takes only a plain dice value, so his Movement is 2D6 and his Movement rule holds both numbers.
- Flag (p3): Goblin Swabbies replace Goblin Warriors at 12 gc and never gain experience, so unlike Da Mob's Goblin Warriors they may not be promoted.
- Flag (p2): The Orc list's Toughened leathers are 7 gc, where every other Sartosa list and our catalogue have them at 5 gc: as printed, for both the Orc and the Goblin Swabbie lists.
- Flag (p4): The Booma Pistol is a stub: 20 gc (40 for a brace), Common, Greenskins only, its rules on p4. The Fire Bomb line is 35 gc; ours is 35 + 2D6 gc, Rare 9, which is unchanged.

### Order of the Mare

- Flag (p1): This warband was read from its mordheimer.net page (https://mordheimer.net/docs/warbands/grade-2a-warbands/order-of-the-mare, last updated 12 Oct 2026), not from a PDF: https://www.broheim.net/downloads/warbands/web/Order%20Of%20The%20Mare.pdf answers 404. Every `page` below is 1, the page itself. See the decisions.
- Flag (p1): The page's heroes are the Paragon (1), the Dame of the Mare (1), the Gallant (0-1) and the Esquiresses (0-2); its henchmen the Pilgrims (any number), the Bowmen (0-7), the Redeemed knights (0-5) and the Companion filly (0-1). No henchmen group sizes and no maximum profile are printed, so the warriors take our Human race.
- Flag (p1): An Order of the Mare warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported.
- Flag (p1): The page heads itself "Grade: Heroic" and "Setting: Bretonnia / Mordheim"; our warband type is grade 2a in Mordheim 2a, from mordheimer's own index, and is unchanged.
- Flag (p1): The skill table has no Special column, so the warband has no skill group of its own. The Paragon and the Dame have no Shooting tick and the Esquiresses no Academic or Strength tick: as printed.
- Flag (p1): "Each warband must have one Paragon" and "must include a single Dame of the Mare", so both are 1 exactly. That she is replaced without delay cannot be held as data, so it is the warband rule "A Dame without delay".
- Flag (p1): Knight's virtue is printed as a warband special rule that the Paragon, the Gallant and the Redeemed knights each point at, so it is their own rule and no warband rule: one special, on the three of them.
- Flag (p1): The Lance line is "(Knights only)" and the Paragon's Vow of poverty forbids him a Lance, so the line is the Gallant's and the Redeemed knights'; his rule is kept so the player sees why. The other "Knights only" lines (Heavy armour, Barding, Warhorse) name all three.
- Flag (p1): "Weapons/Armour: Squires may be equipped from the Archer equipment list" is the Esquiresses' line; the page calls them Squires there.
- Flag (p1): The Paragon's Leader rule names "the Questing knight", which is the Paragon.
- Flag (p1): Barding is 30 gc on this list where ours is 80 gc: the line price stands and ours is unchanged. Every other line matches our price (Warhorse 80, Horse 40, Longbow 15).
- Flag (p1): The Blessings of the Mare are a lore of six prayers, numbered 1 to 6 on the page. The Dame's rule calls them prayers, so she is a Priest. The Dame's charger (6) turns her into a warhorse-mounted model with the profile M8 WS4 BS0 S4 T4 W1 I4 A2 Ld7, which the prayer's own words carry; it is no mount of ours.
- Flag (p1): "Horse and rider" halves the price of one horse or warhorse for the Paragon and the Gallant at recruitment. A conditional price cannot be held on a list line, so it stays a warband rule and the lines keep the full price.
- Flag (p1): The page's "A Word from the Author", "About Balance" and lore sections are flavour and are not imported.

- Price row (p5): our Dragon Sword for pirates-of-the-cathayan-sea, Rare 10.

- Flag (p1): The Pirate column is the Pirate Skills list of the Sartosa campaign rules (Sartosa Skills, Hired Swords and Dramatis Personae, p1-2), not this PDF: it is the shared Sartosa Pirate Skills group, declared in sartosan-pirates.json and named by each hero who ticks it.
- Flag (p2): A stray "Toughened Leathers . . . 5 gc" line sits above the Gunner Equipment List heading, in the right column and under no heading. Both the Pirate list and the Gunner list already sell Toughened Leathers at 5 gc, so it is left out.
- Flag (p5): The Katana (20 gc, Rare 10; close combat, As user +1, Two-handed and Parry) is our Dragon Sword, which carries "In some older material, the Dragon Sword was referred to as Katana" and holds the same price, rarity and rules: the lines link it and show it as Katana, and the warband gets a price row of its own at Rare 10. Cathayan Plate Armour (120 gc, Rare 11; a 4+ save and -1 Movement with a shield) is a stub.
- Flag (p2): The Quarterstaff, Chain sticks, Cathayan Candles, Fish-Hook Shot and Swivel Gun with its three ammunitions are ours already, shown under this list's names. The Monk list sells Throwing Stars at 10 gc, where the Pirate list sells Throwing Stars/Knives at 15 gc: both are our Throwing Knives/Stars, each at its own line's price.
- Flag (p2): The Cathayan Candles line is "25+D6 gc". A list line carries only a whole number, so it is 25 gc; our item is 25 + D6 gc, Rare 9, which is unchanged and is what the store charges.
- Flag (p2): The Pirate list's Heavy Armour is 50 gc, as ours is; the Sartosa Equipment PDF's trading list prints Heavy Armour at 50 gc Rare 7, where ours is Common (open decision 20).
- Flag (p4): Floordogs are taken by Special Recruitment and no hire price is printed, so their cost is 0 gc.
- Flag (p1): The Dragon Monk replaces a Shanghai'er, so the warband holds at most 5 heroes; our templates hold no replacement rule, so the four counts stand as printed (1 + 2 + 1 + 2).
- Flag (p1): A Cathay Pirate warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported. No henchmen group sizes and no maximum profile are printed: the crew takes our Human race.
- Flag (p4): The Martial Artist's Agile (he counts as carrying a Rope & Hook and re-rolls failed Initiative tests for climbing and leaping) is not the Grace of Shornaal mutation Agile (+1 to Initiative tests), so it is kept apart as "Agile (Martial Artist)". The Dragon Monk's rule Art of Silent Death and the special skill Art of the Silent Death are worded differently and do different things (the rule is about a quarterstaff, the skill about an extra attack), so both are kept.
- Flag (p3): The Litter Bearer is listed under the Heroes, with a row in the skill table, and starts with 0 experience: as printed.
- Flag (p14): Sartosa Errata p14: "the Pirate Equipment List for all nationalities (including Cathayan) now has Handgun added on to it, making it available to all members of each warband" - a Handgun line at the setting's own 35 gc.

### Protectorate Of Sigmar

- Alias (p9): "light armor" is our light armour.
- Alias (p9): "heavy armor" is our heavy armour.
- Stub: Blessed Bolts, 25 gc, Common; its rules are on p9.
- Stub: Shield of Sigmar, 20 gc, Rare 9; its rules are on p9.
- Flag (p8): The warband is the issue's pages 8 to 12 (Letters of the Damned #3); the rest of the issue is other articles.
- Flag (p8): A Protectorate warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported. No maximum is printed, so the warriors take our Human race.
- Flag (p8): "You may chose to replace one Templar with a Huntsman": our counts can't tie one fighter's count to another's, so it is the Huntsman's rule.
- Flag (p9): The skill table, read from the layout text: Warrior Priest Combat, Academic, Strength, Speed; Templar Combat, Strength, Speed; Acolyte Combat, Academic, Strength, Speed; Huntsman Combat, Shooting. It has no Special column, and the special skills (p12) don't say who may take them: every Hero ticks Special, two skills being the Warrior Priest's only.
- Flag (p9): "Shield/Buckler 5 gc" is one line for two items: it is two lines, Shield and Buckler, both 5 gc.
- Flag (p9): The Huntsman buys only the lines marked *: Dagger, Sword, Longbow and Blessed Bolts, his own list.
- Flag (p9): Stubs: Blessed Bolts (25 gc, Common; range and Strength as the weapon, +1 S against Undead, mutants, Possessed and other chaotic beings) and the Shield of Sigmar (20 gc, Rare 9; armour save 6, a special 6+ save against all ranged attacks, and no -1 for a shield with heavy armour). Holy Water (5 gc) is our Blessed Water, shown as Holy Water (William, 6 Nov 2026: "yes"), and the Warhammer (15 gc) our Sigmarite Warhammer, shown as Warhammer ("i believe it is the same yes, that warband is in mordheimer"). The Holy Relic is our Holy (Unholy) Relic.
- Flag (p11): No group sizes are printed.
- Flag (p12): The special skills are a new group, Protectorate of Sigmar. Protection of Sigmar and Utter Determination are ours, name and effect; our Utter Determination says "Only the Matriarch or the Warrior Priest" (William, 6 Nov 2026: "Just have one utter determination and say matriarch/warrior priest"). Unshakeable Faith (immune to fear) is the Vampire Hunters' Iron Will, one skill named Iron Will in both groups ("yes go for iron will"); it differs from our Fearless (fear and All Alone). Rousing Sermon and Sigmar's Guidance (printed "Sigmars Guidance") are like nothing of ours.

- Flag (p1): The Pirate column is the Pirate Skills list of the Sartosa campaign rules (Sartosa Skills, Hired Swords and Dramatis Personae, p1-2), not this PDF: it is the shared Sartosa Pirate Skills group, declared in sartosan-pirates.json and named by each hero who ticks it.
- Flag (p10): The Sartosa campaign rules' Equipment PDF (p10) prints one trading list for the whole setting. William, 11 Nov 2026 (decision 20): it goes in as price rows for the Sartosa warband types, and our own prices and rarities never change. Each row below is one the setting's shop prices differently from ours; the list is declared here once, for all twelve Sartosa warband types, and a warband's own list price still wins for that warband. Throwing Axes get no row: they are our Throwing Knives/Stars/Axes, 15 gc Rare 5 for everyone already (William, 11 Nov 2026).
- Flag (p10): The setting's Equipment PDF prints eighteen items our catalogue lacks: they are stubs with the rules and prices it prints, one row for the setting's twelve warband types (William, 11 Nov 2026: "add them in as stubs if you dont have the rules for them yet, we will populate later but good to know they exist"). The Quarantine Flag is 40+2D6 on the trading chart and 30+2D6 in its entry: the entry's, as William settled for the Horn of the Wild Hunt. Each of the six Banners carries the shared Pirate Banner rule and its own. The Greatcoat (10 gc, Common) is our Rain Coat, "Based on 'Raincoat' from Border Town Burning" and the same price, so it is no new item: it is a tag on ours, and the PDF adds that it may be worn over armour without penalty but not with a cloak, and does not keep powder dry when walking through water. The Powder Monkey has no rules in either PDF read.
- Flag (p14): Sartosa Errata p14: "the Pirate Equipment List for all nationalities (including Cathayan) now has Handgun added on to it, making it available to all members of each warband" - a Handgun line at the setting's own 35 gc.
- Flag (p11): Sartosa Errata p11-13: the Sword Pistol is revised (Cost 40 gc, Rare 10, and the rules printed here in place of its own), the Pocket Pistol gains Easy to Use, every banner takes the reworded shared banner rule (Heroes only, no Henchman, one per warband, Navy warbands only the Nation's Colors), the 'Friendly' Flag grants the leader Tactician instead of its own rule, and Powder Monkey is renamed Refined Powder at 10 gc Rare 5 with the rules the errata prints ("'Powder Monkey' is removed from the Trading List (this was the original name of 'Refined Powder')").
- Flag (p6): Sartosa Add On p6-7 (NEW EQUIPMENT) gives the setting two items we lacked: the Stegadon Gun (200 gc, Rare 9 in Sartosa) and the Trained Monkey (a beast in apps/bestiary/data/bestiary.json, as the Wardogs are). It also prices the Fire Bomb we already hold at Rare 7 in Sartosa, where ours is Rare 9 for everyone, so the setting takes a row of its own; the PDF's "except for Shadow Elves, Wood Elves, Forest Goblins, and Savage Orcs" leaves the Savage Orcs off that row. The Add On's Dread Ship (+25% of a boat's cost, -1 Movement, Evil warbands only) is a change to a boat rather than an item with a price of its own, so it is flagged for William, not imported.
- Flag (p2): Sartosa Skills PDF p2, SKILLS ADDITION: "The skill 'Knife Thrower' may now be used with 'Belaying Pins'. It may not be used with Throwing Axes." Our skill of that rule is Knife-Fighter, a Mordheim 1a skill many warbands learn, so the addition sits on the setting's own Belaying Pins price row rather than in the skill's description. The same page's NEW SHOOTING SKILL, Axe Thrower, is already in our Shooting group as Sartosa's.
- Flag (p10): Sartosa Equipment p10 prices the Hunting Rifle at 200 gc Rare 9 where ours is 200 gc Rare 11, so the setting takes a row of its own. Its "Double-barreled Hunting Rifle 300 gc Rare 11" is flagged instead: we hold two items of that profile (the Ostlander's and Mutiny in Marienburg's, both Rare 12) and which one the Sartosa list means is a question for William.

### Savage Orcs (Athel Loren)

- Stub: Magic War Paint, 10 gc, Rare 9; its rules are on p48.
- Flag (p47): A Savage Orc warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported.
- Flag (p47): The Animosity table prints its three results as 1, 2 and 3, but result 1 says the warrior "behaves as if a 2-5 had been rolled on this chart", and no result is printed for 4-6. The three results are imported as 1, 2-5 and 6, which is what result 1's own wording and the rulebook's chart give. Decision 1.
- Flag (p48): The skill table ticks the Shaman Combat, Strength and Speed, and the Black Orc and the Scarboy Combat and Strength. Read from the tick positions against the column centres. The Shaman is a wizard but is not ticked Academic; taken as printed. Decision 2.
- Flag (p48): There is no Special column, so the warband has no special skill group. Our existing "Savage Orcs" skill group is the Sartosa warband's and is not given to this one.
- Flag (p48): The Orc Equipment List's Armour heading has a stray "25gc" beside it at the cost column. No item carries that price; the four armour lines under it are Light Armour 20, Heavy Armour 50, Shield 5 and Helmet 10, all imported as printed.
- Flag (p48): "Missile Weapons*" in the Savage Orc list is marked Forest Goblins only (Short Bow). "Missile Weapons**" in the Orc list is marked Orcs only (Bow, Crossbow); both fighters who buy from that list are Orcs, so no note was added. Heavy Armour is marked Black Orcs only.
- Flag (p49): The Shaman's "may take magic warpaint, but may not take shields" is kept both as a note on the Shield line and as his own rule, since he and three other fighters share the list.
- Flag (p48): Boar is 60 gc on the equipment list; the Orc Camp and Orc Black Market price charts print 40 + 10D6 gc. The list price is used, as a list price. It is our War Boar, bought under the PDF's name.
- Flag (p38): Magic War Paint is a stub with no rules: the warband pages print none and the campaign's own equipment pages were not read. Its rarity is the Orc Camp chart's Rare 9; the Orc Black Market chart prints Rare 5 for the same item. Decision 3.
- Flag (p50): Only the Black Orc carries a Leader rule, and he is 0-2, so a warband may be raised with no leader. The Shaman is the one mandatory hero but the PDF gives him no leadership rule. Decision 4.
- Flag (p52): Weedy Grots makes a Forest Goblin re-roll any "Lad's Got Talent", so the group is imported as not promotable; the rule's own words are kept on him.
- Flag (p48): "Your warband may never include more Forest Goblins than Orcs" is kept as the Forest Goblin's own rule, since our templates hold no ratio between two fighters.
- Flag (p49): Waaagh! Magic is our "Waaaagh! Magic" lore. The PDF points at Town Cryer 6 for the spell list and prints none of its own.
- Flag (p51): Henchmen are "bought in groups of 1-5", imported as the group size for every henchman group.
- Flag (p50): Savage ("he is subject to frenzy") is our shared Frenzy special under another name, and "Savage" is now one of Frenzy's names, so both fighters link the shared special (William, 12 Nov 2026). The Black Orcs' Savage, which does far more than frenzy, stays their own. Decision 15.
- Flag (p48): Karak Azgal's Savage Orcs learn from our rulebook "Orcs" group; this warband's skill table has no Special column at all, so it learns from no warband group and shares none. Decision 17.
- Flag (p38): Karak Azgal's Savage Orcs brought a Warpaint item (Miscellaneous, 5 gc, Rare 6, "Mork Protects": a one-use 6+ invulnerable save). This warband's Magic War Paint is an Armour entry at 10 gc, Rare 9, whose rules this PDF does not print, and the Shaman may take it in place of a shield, which reads as a standing save rather than a one-use one. Kept apart until its rules are known. Decision 18.
- Flag (p38): Checked against the grade 3 batch's thirteen stubs as well: none of them is war paint of any kind, so Magic War Paint stays the one open question beside Karak Azgal's Warpaint. Decision 18.

### Savage Orcs

- Flag (p5): This warband is the second half of the Orc Pirates & Savage Orcs PDF (p5-7), "the exact same rules as the Da Mob Warband with the following changes": William, 11 Nov 2026: "Orc Pirates & Savage Orcs should just be 2 variants of the orc mob if that still works, correctly pointing at their settings". So this page is not a warband list of its own: it folds into the Orc Mob's template as one of its variants (`variantOf`), which serves the Orc Mob (Mordheim 1a), the Orc Pirates and the Savage Orcs (Sartosa), each type presetting its own variant. Its rules are its variant's, its lists' lines name it, and its fighters are the variant's: one Da Mob lacks joins the roster and no other variant allows any of him, and one it lacks is allowed none in this variant.
- Flag (p5): The warband prints no roster of its own, so its fighters are Da Mob's with their costs, profiles, starting experience and counts: the Boss, the Shaman, the Big 'Uns, the Orc Boyz, the Goblins, the Cave Squigs and the Troll. The Goblins are named Forest Goblins, as the equipment list and Wild Animosity name them. Distasteful Company is Da Mob's rule and stands.
- Flag (p5): Wild Animosity rolls for every standing warrior not in combat, heroes and the Troll included (the Troll not while he has failed his stupidity test), and every warrior rolls rather than only those who roll a 1.
- Flag (p6): The Savage Orc Equipment List's "Throwing Axe" is our Throwing Knives/Stars/Axes, the one entry for all of them (William, 11 Nov 2026), 15 gc Rare 5 for everyone, shown under the list's name. The Axe Thrower rule the PDF prints (two axes a shooting phase at +1 Strength, not with Quickshot or Knife Thrower) is our Axe Thrower skill already.
- Flag (p6): The Forest Goblin list sells a Short Bow at 10 gc where our catalogue has it at 5 gc: the line keeps its own price. Its "Throwing weapons . . . 15 gc" names no weapon; it is read as our Throwing Knives/Stars/Axes, which that price matches.
- Flag (p6): Page 6 carries a block of the designers' discussion below the equipment lists ("Wardance - could end up in the same place you were before..."), which is not rules and is not imported.
- Flag (p5): The PDF prints no Pirate column and no Pirate skill list, so this warband does not join the shared Sartosa Pirate Skills group.
- Flag (p6): The Troll's Regeneration (a 4+ to ignore a wound) is not the Bounty of Arkhar mutation Regeneration (heal to full Wounds), so it is kept apart as "Regeneration (Troll)", as Da Mob's is.

### Sea Ghosts

- Flag (p3): A Sea Elf Rangers warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported. No henchmen group sizes are printed. No maximum profile is printed: the Elves take our Elf race.
- Flag (p3): The skill table's Special tick for the Wayfinder is a glyph the text layer drops: the table was read from p3's image.
- Flag (p3): River Boat ("may include one River Boat") is read as the list's Riverboat (100 gc): one per warband.
- Flag (p3): Sword/Glaive is our Sword shown as Sword/Glaive; Spear-stave (counts as Halberd) our Halberd shown as Spear-stave. The Feast-Master's Double-handed weapon is 10 gc (15 on the Elf Rangers list): as printed. The Ithilmar weapon is 3 x price.
- Flag (p3): Boat hook (8 gc), Belaying pin (3), Sea Dragon cloak (50), Elven wine (30) and Compass (45) are ours by name (the Pirates' and Dark Elves' items); the PDF prints no rules for them.
- Flag (p4): The Lyre is our Lyre: 15 gc, Rare 8, Minstrels only, as its entry prints. With Lyres the Minstrels raise the warband's most warriors by 1: not imported, the most stays 12.
- Flag (p5): The Wayfinder's profile row reads Shipmaster (his Navigator rule), the Lookouts' Sentinel. The PDF doesn't say how a Wayfinder earns his captaincy.
- Flag (p5): The Wardancer Dances (Whirling Death, Storm of Blades, The Shadows Coil, Woven Mist) are no skills of ours, and a page has one Special group, so they are in the Feast-Master's Shadow Dances of Loec rule; the player notes a Dance learned.
- Flag (p6): Animal Charmer ("eg. Falcon Charmer"): the warband has no animals to hire. Kept as printed.
- Flag (p6): The special skills are our Sea Ghosts group's six. Our Hide in Shadows rounds the halved Initiative up, where the PDF says nothing of rounding; our Powerful Build also bars Shadow Weavers. Ours are unchanged.

### Shallows Beasts

- Flag (p2): The PDF calls the warband Stromfels Reavers, or the Cult of Stromfels. It needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported. No henchmen group sizes and no maximum are printed, so it takes our Human race.
- Flag (p2): The skill table has no Special column, so the warband has no skill group. Its ticks are read from where the text layer places them: Buccaneer all five; Mutant-Priest Combat, Academic, Speed; Renegade Combat, Shooting, Strength, Speed; Foundling Combat, Speed.
- Flag (p1): Aquatic Mutants is a tailored upgrade list, Aquatic Mutants, one each for Heroes at its price: Blackblood 30, Great Claw 50, Tentacle 35, Prehensile Tail 50, Beak 35, Electrical Touch 45, Mer-creature 25, Suckers 25, Hideous 40 ("eye stalks (counts as hideous)"). The heroes' costs say "+ the cost of mutation".
- Flag (p2): Club/Mace/Staff and Club/Mace are our Club, Mace or Hammer, and Cutlass/Sword our Sword, each shown under the PDF's name.
- Flag (p3): The fighters' headings aren't in the text layer, so each is named from Choice of Warriors and his profile row (Fishmen's reads Mermen), and each cost is the one in his column: Buccaneer 85 gc, Mutant-Priest 75, Renegade 35, Foundling 20, Wrecker 20, Swamper 25, Fishman 30.
- Flag (p3): The Mutant-Priest is a wizard of the Chaos Rituals (ours) and may also use the Prayers of Manann and take a Mark of Manann (the Miracle Workers chapter), which we lack: only the Chaos Rituals are set, and his Prayers rule is kept as printed.
- Flag (p3): The Mutant-Priest's Bite Attack does what our shared Beak mutation does, under another name: kept his own rule.
- Flag (p4): The Fishmen's Mer-creatures is our shared Mer-creature but for its "on land its Movement is reduced by 1": kept apart, as Mer-creature (Fishmen).
- Flag (p5): The Stromfels Heart, Shark-tooth Necklace and Scrimshaw Bauble are our MiM items, with the same prices and rarities. Ours carry no rules, so the PDF's are attached, in our words; the Scrimshaw Bauble's rule has no name, as the PDF gives none.
- Flag (p5): Our Stromfels Heart's price row said "Priest of Stromfels only"; the PDF's entry says "Stromfels Reavers only" and lets any Hero use it, so the row is now the Shallows Beasts' Heroes' (no other warband buys it). The Harbinger's Massive Beak (Mordheim Annual p35), the Altar to Stromfels, the Ceremony of Sacrifice and the Conviction chart are Mutiny in Marienburg rules we don't have.

Our mounts are bestiary animals, so the stub is a plain item with neither; it sits in a Special equipment list every fighter with a list buys from, as the snapshot's special equipment does.

### Skaven of Clan Pestilens (Lustria)

- Price row (p1): our censer for skaven-of-clan-pestilens-lus, Rare 12, Plague Champions only.
- Flag (p1): A Clan Pestilens warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported.
- Flag (p1): The Skaven maximum profile printed (M6 WS6 BS6 S4 T4 W3 I7 A4 Ld7) is our plain Skaven race's, not our Skaven (Clan Pestilens) race's (M5 T5) which the Mordheim 1b and Mousillon warbands of the same name use. Imported as printed, so this warband's race is Skaven.
- Flag (p1): The warband has no Special Rules section of its own: the PDF prints none.
- Flag (p2): The skill table ticks the Plague Priest Combat, Academic, Strength, Speed and Special; the Plague Champions Combat, Strength, Speed and Special; and the Monk Initiates Combat, Speed and Special. Read from the tick positions.
- Flag (p2): The special skills are a new group, Clan Pestilens (Lustria). Cloud of Flies and Frenzy are ours, with the same words. Resilience (+1 Toughness) is a skill of its own: our Resilient takes -1 Strength off hits in close combat, which is a different rule. No Pain (stunned counts as knocked down) is our Ignore Pain and Fear (causes fear) is our Fearsome: both are merged into ours (William, 12 Nov 2026), so the group learns ours and the PDF's names are dropped. Ignore Pain keeps its own wording with its "only with the Resilient Strength skill" condition dropped, since this warband's skill carries no such condition.
- Flag (p1): The Plague Censer is our Censer. The equipment list sells it at 100 gc, a starting price, and its own entry prices it at 75+5D6, Rare 12, Plague Champions only, which is the price row. The Mousillon warband's censer is an item of its own, Plague Censer (Mousillon), because its Fog of Death rolls a D6 for every model in base contact at the end of each close-combat phase; this PDF's takes a Toughness test from the model it hits, which is what our Censer does, so this warband buys ours.
- Flag (p1): This PDF's Fog of Death adds an extra -1 to hit for shooting at the wielder and says a roll of 6 always wounds whatever the Toughness. Our Censer takes the same Toughness test without those two lines; ours is unchanged.
- Flag (p2): The Hero list's dagger is priced "1st free/2 wt" in warp tokens where every other line is in gold crowns; the PDF treats warp tokens as gold crowns, so it is imported as 2 gc.
- Flag (p2): The Hero list's Staff (3 gc) and the Henchmen list's Club/Staff (3 gc) are our Club, Mace or Hammer, bought under the PDF's names.
- Flag (p2): The Henchmen list's Flail is noted "Plague Monks only".
- Flag (p3): The Plague Priest's Leader rule names the Loremaster, which belongs to another warband's PDF; it is read as the Plague Priest.
- Flag (p3): The Monk Initiate is a hero at 15 gc with 0 starting experience.
- Flag (p4): The Clan Rats have no maximum ("any number"). No henchmen group sizes are printed, and no fighter is said to gain no experience, so every henchman gains it. The Skaven Slave gains experience but may never be promoted: his Doomed rule removes him instead.
- Flag (p1): Our catalogue already holds two Skaven of Clan Pestilens templates, the Mordheim 1b warband's and Mousillon's, and a template is kept by its name, so this one is named Skaven of Clan Pestilens (Lustria). Its skills, profiles and prices differ from both: it shares only the skills Cloud of Flies and Frenzy with the Mousillon group, and it buys our Censer where Mousillon has an item of its own.

### Skaven of Clan Pestilens (Mousillon)

- Stub: Plague Censer (Mousillon), 75+5D6 gc, Rare 12; its rules are on p4.
- Flag (p2): A Clan Pestilens warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported. No henchmen group sizes and no maximum profiles are printed.

The Champion's rule is printed in the Priest's words ("The Plague Priest is a disease-ridden Skaven"), read as the Champion's own.
- Flag (p4): The Plague Censer is priced 100 wt on the equipment list and 75+5D6 wt, Rare 12, in its own entry: the list price is the starting price and the entry's is the item's price row.
- Flag (p4): The Plague Censer is its own item, Plague Censer (Mousillon), with the PDF's rules: As user +2, Heavy, Two Handed and a Fog of Death that rolls a D6 for every model in base contact at the end of each close-combat phase (a 6 wounds, no armour save, the wielder immune) and takes -1 to hit when shooting at the wielder. Our Censer (Town Cryer 29) keeps its own Fog of Death, a Toughness test, and is unchanged; this one takes the setting's name, since three warbands are called Skaven of Clan Pestilens (William, 11 Nov 2026).
- Flag (p3): The equipment lists' Staff (3 gc) and Club/Staff (3 gc) are our Club, Mace or Hammer, bought under the PDF's names.
- Flag (p3): The Clanrat list's Flail is noted "Plague Monks and Initiates only": read as Plague Monks and Monk Initiates.
- Flag (p2): The special skills are a new group, Clan Pestilens (Mousillon). Our Skaven Clan Pestilens group (the Mordheim 1b warband's) holds different skills, so the two are kept apart. Resilient is ours. Rotten Body (causes fear) is our Fearsome and Numb is our Ignore Pain: both are merged into ours (William, 12 Nov 2026), so the group learns ours and the PDF's names are dropped. Our own Rotten Body, which makes a Skaven immune to poisons, diseases and the fog of death, is a different rule and keeps the name. Cloud of Flies does what the shared special of that name does, under another name: it is kept apart.
- Flag (p6): Plague Monks have no maximum ("any number"). Plague Rats and Skaven Slaves gain no experience.
- Flag (p1): Our catalogue already holds a Skaven of Clan Pestilens template, the Mordheim 1b warband's, and a template is kept by its name, so this one is named Skaven of Clan Pestilens (Mousillon). Its warband type is named Skaven of Clan Pestilens, as the PDF names it.
- Flag (p7): Page 7 holds only the Skaven Slave's Abused rule; the measured scan page carries nothing else.

- Flag (p67): A Clan Skryre warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported.
- Flag (p68): The skill table ticks the Warlock Engineer Shooting, Academic, Speed and Special, the Stormvermin Combat, Strength, Speed and Special, and the Warlock Apprentice Shooting, Speed and Special. Read from the tick positions against the column centres.
- Flag (p68): Tail Fighting, Wall Runner and Infiltration are ours with the same words. Infiltration's "available to Stormvermin only" and Skavenbrew Master's "Warlock Engineers or Apprentices only" are in the group's note, not in a warband rule.
- Flag (p68): This is not Karak Azgal's shared "Skaven of Karak Azgal" group (the rulebook Skaven skills less the Art of Silent Death) nor our rulebook "Skaven" group: it has no Black Hunger and adds Skavenbrew Master and Warpstone Resistance. Kept apart as "Skaven Clan Skryre (Athel Loren)". Decision 11.
- Flag (p69): The Engineers list prices a Helmet at 5 gc and the Henchmen list at 10 gc; both are imported as their list prints them.
- Flag (p69): The Warplock Pistol line reads "30gc (70 brace)", so a brace is not twice the single price. Both numbers are imported as printed. The Clan Skryre Armoury chart prints 35 gc (70 brace) for the same weapon. Decision 12.
- Flag (p70): Warplock Pistol is our Warplock pistol, with the same 8" range, Strength 5, Prepare Shot and -3 save modifier; ours adds its close combat use, which this PDF does not print.
- Flag (p70): Poison Wind Globes is not our Poison Wind Globe: ours carries the Relics of the Crusades wording (6", Strength Special, Thrown Weapon, Poison wind - it ignores cover penalties and armour saves, does D3 wounds on a 3+ and one wound on a 4+ to anyone within 2", and is magical, so it affects undead - and Drop, a 1 to hit dropping it on the thrower), where this PDF prints a different mechanism: 6", Strength 4, No Armour Save, Template (every model within 1" of the point hit takes a Strength 4 hit, so a model up to 7" from the thrower may be hit, and a miss deviates the template D3" in a random direction) and Fragile (if the carrier is knocked down or stunned, a D6 of 1 bursts a globe, resolved as though thrown at a point centred on him). The rules really differ, so this one is an item of its own, Poison Wind Globe (Athel Loren), at this list's 50 gc, Rare 6, and the lines and the special equipment entry name it (William, 12 Nov 2026); ours keeps the plain name, as the earlier of the two. Decision 13.
- Flag (p70): Warplock Jezzail, Warpfire Thrower, Warpstone Armour, Jezzail Support, Warpfire Barrel, Warpstone Shard and Skavenbrew are new items, each with the rules this PDF prints. Our Fog-enhancing Warpstone Shards and Warpstone Amulet are different items.
- Flag (p74): Warpstone Shard and Skavenbrew are on no equipment list: the Clan Skryre Armoury chart sells them and the PDF prints their rules, so each is a stub whose one price row serves this warband type.
- Flag (p71): The Warpstone Shard's own text repeats the Poison Wind Globes paragraph before its rules; only its rules were taken, since they are what it does.
- Flag (p75): The Warlock Apprentice begins with no spells, so he carries no Wizard rule, but the Magic of the Horned Rat is offered to him so the spell picker holds it when Trainees gives him one. Decision 14.
- Flag (p77): The Slave's "may carry shields, but may not wear helmets or light armour" is both a note on those two lines and his own rule, since the Clanrats share the list.
- Flag (p67): "Any number of Slaves, up to a maximum of half your warband" is a warband rule: our templates hold no ratio between a fighter and the warband's size.
- Flag (p77): Clanrats and Slaves have no maximum ("any number"). Every henchman group is "bought in groups of 1-5".
- Flag (p66): The setting's own shop price charts (pages 38 to 46) re-price and re-rarify our items per shop location, not per warband, so they are not imported; the stubs' rarities are the Clan Skryre Armoury's. Decision 6.
- Flag (p68): Karak Azgal's shared "Skaven of Karak Azgal" group is Black Hunger, Tail Fighting, Wall Runner and Infiltration, and its Clan Skryre has no Special column of its own. This warband's Special column holds Tail Fighting, Wall Runner, Infiltration, Skavenbrew Master and Warpstone Resistance: no Black Hunger and two skills of its own, so the shared group is not used. Decision 11.
- Flag (p70): Karak Azgal's Clan Skryre brought a Jezzail (36", S5, Move or fire, Prepare shot, -3 save, Pick target, a two-Skaven crew with an assistant and a pavise), 175 gc Rare 11, and the Relics of the Crusades batch left it alone (its own Clan Skryre sells no jezzail). This warband's Warplock Jezzail is the same range and Strength but is -2 to hit (Difficult to use) until a jezzail support steadies it, and has no crew, pick-target or pavise rules, so it is kept apart. Decision 16.
- Flag (p74): The Relics of the Crusades Clan Skryre brought a Warpstone Token (a wizard eats one before casting for +1 to the roll, cumulative, and a double takes him out of action). This warband's Warpstone Shard is a Warlock Engineer only, takes 2 off the spell's difficulty, is one use and then stuns him on a D6 of 4+, so the two are kept apart. Decision 19.
- Flag (p74): Checked against the grade 3 batch's thirteen stubs as well: none of them is a warpstone item, so the Warpstone Shard's only near neighbour is still the Relics Warpstone Token. Decision 19.

- Flag (p2): A troupe must hold at least one Fight Master and no more than two, so the group is imported as 1-2.
- Flag (p6): The Marksman Equipment List is headed "for Marksmen only" but the PDF has no Marksman; the Forest Kin are the only fighters sent to it, so it is theirs.
- Flag (p6): The Rapier is 15 gc here and ours is 15 gc Rare 5 but open to eight other warbands, so the troupe gets a price row. The Duelling pistol is 25 gc (50 for a brace) against our 30 (60), and the Hunting rifle, Blunderbuss, Handgun and Pistol are ours at the same prices.
- Flag (p6): "Mace/Club" is read as our Mace, "Spear/Pitchfork" as our Spear, "Halberd/Bill/Scythe" as our Halberd and "Helmet/Pot" as our Helmet, each shown under the list's own wording.
- Flag (p7): The Thespian spell list is imported as the lore Thespian Magic: Scripted 9, Soliloquy 11, Our Story Thus Far 10. The Narrator casts from it or from Lesser Magic, as the PDF allows.
- Flag (p2): No group sizes are printed except the Townsfolk's 2-5.

### Turjuk

- Flag (p2): The PDF prints one Arabian Tribes warband list (pages 2-4) with three tribes - Ghutani, Muzil and Turjuk - that share its choice of warriors, fighters, profiles, prices and equipment lists and differ only in their Special Rules and their skill table. Our data holds the three as three warband types, so each is a source file of its own, the shared parts repeated and the differences carried.
- Flag (p2): An Arabian Tribe warband needs at least 3 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported.
- Flag (p3): The three skill tables' ticks are glyphs the reading-order text drops; read from the -layout text's tick columns and checked against page 3 as an image. The tables have five columns and no Special column, so the tribes have no skill group of their own.
- Flag (p3): The Arabian Tribe and Townsmen lists print Mace and Hammer as two lines at 3 dinars each; both are our Club, Mace or Hammer, so each list carries one line for them.
- Flag (p4): The Scholar's Theology field gives him Holy skills, and the Occult field makes him a Lesser Magic wizard. We hold no Holy skill group and a field is picked per model, so neither is a skill group or a spell lore on the Scholar: the choice stays in his rule.
- Flag (p4): No maximum profile and no henchmen group sizes are printed: the warband takes our Human race.
- Flag (p2): "Every Turjuk Emir and Askar has the following skills: Ride Horse, Ride Arabian Steed and Horse Archer" names skills, so both start with them. Ride Horse and Ride Arabian Steed are our one Ride [Animal] skill, which names its animal, so each starts with Ride [Animal] and Horse Archer.
- Flag (p2): The Arabian Steed is named in the Turjuk rules but is in no equipment list and no profile or price is printed for it, so no item or beast is added.

### Underworld Alliance

- Flag (p1): An Underworld warband needs at least 4 models: our templates hold no minimum, so it is not imported. No henchmen group sizes are printed.
- Flag (p1): The warband has two leaders, the Goblin Bully and the Slum Lord, each with Leader for his own race only (An Alliance of Inconvenience); the template marks both as leaders.
- Flag (p1): Warpstone for Breakfast is a tailored upgrade list, for Heroes at each mutation's price: Poisonous Bite 50, Extra Arm 40, Hulking Brute 50, Regeneration 60, Suckers 25, Elastic Limbs 40, Vestigial Twin 60, Cloud of Flies 25. The PDF's "Elastic Arms" is read as our Elastic Limbs. A leader may take two, so the list sets no limit for each fighter.
- Flag (p2): The skill table's ticks are symbol glyphs, read from where the text layer places them: Bully Combat, Shooting, Academic, Speed, Special; Slum Lord Combat, Academic, Strength, Speed, Special; Slave Champion Combat, Speed, Special; Stinky Git Shooting, Speed, Special. The leaders' starred Academic ticks are the warband rule Jealousy.
- Flag (p2): The Man Catcher is "Leader only" on both lists: the Goblin Bully's on the Greenskin list, the Slum Lord's on the Skaven list. Light Armor is our Light Armour, Madcap Mushrooms our Mad Cap Mushrooms (30 gc) at the list's 15 gc. The Miscellaneous Items "available to all heroes" are a list every Hero buys from.
- Flag (p2): The special skills are a new group, Underworld Alliance. Infiltration ("same as the skaven skill") is ours.
- Flag (p3): The Slum Lord's profile row and rules call him the Slave King: one fighter, named the Slum Lord as Choice of Warriors names him.
- Flag (p4): Boglars have no maximum printed, and "share characteristics" of the Gnoblars and trolls: they take our Gnoblar race. Their Regeneration (5+) and the Warpstone Troll's (4+) are each kept apart from our shared Regeneration, and from the Trolls' (who also never roll for injury).
- Flag (p4): Sewer Squigs are 0-5 in their heading but "up to 4" in Choice of Warriors: the heading is kept. Skaven Slaves use the Skaven list, Boglars the Greenskin list.
- Flag (p5): The Warpstone Troll is one model (0-1). The PDF mentions its upkeep but prints no price: not imported. Its Large Target is our Large.

## Pointer rules and starting skills

A fighter's or dramatis persona's own rule that only pointed to a skill, or said he has one, is now the skill, which he starts with as one he'd learned (William, 7 Nov 2026: "yes please do that"; catalogue_import/pointer_rules.py), and the rule goes; a rule that says more keeps its text:

- Battle Monks of Cathay: Emissary: Ride Horse, starts with Ride [Animal].
- Bretonnian Chapel Guard: Questing Knight: Ride, starts with Ride [Animal].
- Carnival of Chaos: Brute: Unnatural Strength, starts with Strongman.
- Estalian Corsairs: Pirate Captain: Expert Swordsman, starts with Expert Swordsman.
- Estalian Corsairs: Ship's Mate: Expert Swordsman, starts with Expert Swordsman.
- Forest Goblins: Chieftain: Ride Spiders, starts with Ride [Animal].
- Gunnery School of Nuln: Senior Gunnery Officer: Hunter, starts with Hunter.
- Gunnery School of Nuln: Marksmen: Quick Reload, starts with Hunter.
- Imperial Outriders: Knight: Ride, starts with Ride [Animal].
